HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001","root_000355/B002","root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1506bee337c6ae54117f","text":"1. **Praise as an evaluative verdict after testing the order.** Lexical evidence: ح م د B001, praise opposite blame; ح م د B002, finding something satisfactory after testing; ع ل م B001, knowing as clarity. Structural cue: praise follows the named mercy and precedes the list of lordship, judgment, worship, and guidance. Assigned role: الحمد is both spoken praise and a judgment that the whole disclosed order proves praiseworthy. Abductive move: the packet gives praise/testing/clarity; I infer that the following ayat function as the evidentiary field that makes praise intelligible. Reading change: “all praise” reads less like a formula and more like the verdict produced by seeing the whole mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_30259d1943ed35451a55","text":"1. **Praise as tested recognition, not only spontaneous acclaim.** Evidence: `ح م د B001` praise/thanksgiving, `ح م د B002` finding something praiseworthy after testing, `ن ع م B001` favor in 1:7, and `غ ض ب B001`/`ض ل ل B001` negative alternatives in 1:7. Mechanism: the later contrast between favored, angered, and lost paths makes الحمد sound like a verdict reached after discriminating between outcomes. The packet supplies praise, favor, anger, and straying; I infer the evaluative testing relation. Abductive move: praise becomes meaningful when the path has alternatives that could have ended in anger or loss. Reading change: “الحمد” reads as thankful recognition after moral-path testing, not only as opening doxology.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3b77d0e7e3ed0aa65500","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:2:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Rablik, konuşanlarla kurulan bağlılığı bir koruma ve güvence ilişkisi olarak da düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_494070ba469a7eb33b41","text":"1:7 sharpened رب العالمين: “favored,” “angered,” and “astray” showed that the worlds are not abstract cosmic plurality but a morally sorted field. The animal/stray branch of ض ل ل B005 also made رب sound unexpectedly like owner-care: the Lord of worlds contrasts with ownerless wandering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001","root_000355/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5343760e418376efaade","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:2:1\",\"root_000355\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki iyilik ve olumsuz yol sonuçları, övgüyü sınanmış sonuçlar arasında verilmiş, minnettarlık taşıyan bir değerlendirmeye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001040/B002","root_001273/B009","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_60e46d8128c8d3a7b68a","text":"2. **Rabb as caretaker who brings many marked worlds to completion.** Evidence: `ر ب ب B002` repair, nurture, and completion; `ع ل م B002` distinguishing mark/landmark; `ه د ي B001` guidance toward way/truth in 1:6; `ق و م B009` support/mainstay in 1:6. Mechanism: “العالمين” is activated by the branch of signs and markers: worlds are readable, marked domains that require ربّ care, and guidance later asks to move through those marks rightly. The packet supplies Rabb, worlds/knowledge-sign branches, guidance, and straightness; I infer that nurture makes the marked order navigable. Abductive move: a ربّ of many worlds is not only owner of plurality but the one who maintains their intelligibility. Reading change: “رب العالمين” becomes a claim about sustaining a legible cosmos, not only possessing all beings.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_001444/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_62fc676087230c7e05dd","text":"3. **`رَبّ` as a title that entails allegiance.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ر ب ب` B001 includes ownership, mastery, and obeyed authority, and fixed `ء ل ه` B001 identifies the worshipped one. Later 1:4 adds `م ل ك` B003, dominion, and `د ي ن` B001, obedience, before 1:5 `ع ب د` B003 enacts submissive worship. **Structural cue:** the sequence moves from third-person title to sovereign title and then to the repeated exclusive object `إياك`. **Role and abductive move:** the titles and service are explicit; I infer that exclusive service is the practical consequence of the lordship claim. **Reading change:** `رب العالمين` is not only a description of scale but a claim on the speakers’ allegiance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000355/B005","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_821ddd203c8fb24223f4","text":"4. **Praise resists benefaction-credit rivalry.** Lexical evidence: ح م د B005, claiming praise by favor; ن ع م B001 in 1:7, bestowed favor; ء ل ه B001, worshipped object. Structural cue: 1:7 mentions favor bestowed by “You,” while 1:5 confines worship and help to “You.” Assigned role: الحمد prevents favor from becoming a human credit-claim. Abductive move: the packet supplies favor and exclusive address; I infer that all benefaction-credit is redirected to Allah. Reading change: praise is not only gratitude; it blocks rival ownership of benefaction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_939d1352a02734594b20","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:2:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"1:2:4\",\"root_001040\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rablik, varlık alanlarını yalnızca sahiplenmek değil, ayırt edici işaretlerini koruyarak aşama aşama yön bulunabilir kılmaktır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9f1df6730140c5cdb296","text":"The unexpected activator was 1:7 `أنعمت`: it retrospectively gave fixed `الحمد` a concrete benefaction to answer and strengthened `ح م د` B001 over a merely generic praise reading. Meanwhile, 1:6–1:7 turned `ع ل م` B002 from a dormant sign image into a route mechanism—worlds under `رب` become legible by distinctions that let travelers recognize a course and its outcomes. 1:4–1:5 also confirmed that fixed lordship develops into allegiance, not scale alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ac4341aa31c833ff9ca1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:2:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rab unvanı, bakımın yanı sıra konuşanların sonraki kulluklarını bağlayan bir yönetim ve bağlılık ilişkisi kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ae726c4cecd4e65326bb","text":"1:6 unexpectedly made `ع ل م B002` important: “worlds” began to look like marked terrain whose signs need guidance, not just a plural totality. 1:7 then fed back into الحمد through `ن ع م B001`; praise became gratitude for favor and also a recognition that some outcomes are not praiseworthy because they end in anger or loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bbe5869faffd0ad23261","text":"2. **Lordship as cultivation of legible worlds.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ر ب ب` B002 is repair, nurture, administration, and completion stage by stage; fixed `ع ل م` B002 is a distinguishing mark, landmark, or sign that points to something. **Structural cue:** the two roots are joined in `رَبِّ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ`, while 1:6 asks for guidance on a road and 1:7 identifies that road through differentiated human outcomes. **Role and abductive move:** `ر ب ب` supplies cultivation and `ع ل م` supplies legibility; I infer that stagewise care makes domains navigable by preserving meaningful distinctions within them. **Reading change:** the phrase depicts ongoing cultivation of orientable, sign-bearing realms rather than static possession of an undifferentiated totality.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d3e9b7124589730467ff","text":"1. **Praise as a grateful verdict grounded in benefit.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ح م د` B001 includes both commendation and thanksgiving for benefaction; B002 adds finding someone or something satisfactory after testing. Later `ن ع م` B001 in 1:7 names bestowed favor, while 1:3 and 1:5 supply mercy and aid. **Structural cue:** praise precedes the petition and its eventual description of favored recipients, and the path’s alternatives provide an evaluative contrast. **Role and abductive move:** favor, mercy, and aid are packet-supplied grounds; I infer that the speaker treats their experienced or anticipated effects as the “test” by which the praised one is found praiseworthy. **Reading change:** `الحمد` becomes an evidence-bearing, grateful judgment, not undirected acclaim.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e066f0bcc574696e14ca","text":"3. **Lordship as authority, covenant, and administered dependence.** Evidence: `ر ب ب B001` lordship/ownership/authority, `ر ب ب B011` covenant or protection pact, `م ل ك B003` kingship/dominion and `د ي ن B001/B002` obedience/reckoning in 1:4, plus `ع ب د B003` worship in 1:5. Mechanism: later obedience, judgment, and worship activate the legal-political side of ربّ: praise is directed to one whose care is also binding authority. The packet supplies authority, covenant, obedience, and worship; I infer that praise acknowledges administered dependence. Abductive move: if the speaker will later declare exclusive service, the ربّ here must already be more than a nurturer; he is the binding governor of the relation. Reading change: the ayah preserves both care and rule, rather than disambiguating Rabb into either tenderness or sovereignty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002","root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f104956827da28262e6a","text":"2. **Lordship as stage-by-stage cultivation of worlds.** Lexical evidence: ر ب ب B001, lordship/ownership/authority; ر ب ب B002, repair, nurture, completion; ع ل م B002, distinguishing signs and road markers. Structural cue: “Lord” is attached to “worlds,” while later 1:6 asks for a road and 1:7 identifies the path by people marked by favor. Assigned role: رب is the maintainer who forms worlds into legible, navigable fields. Abductive move: the packet supplies nurture and markers; I infer that the worlds are not merely objects owned but terrains cultivated into guidance-bearing order. Reading change: “Lord of the worlds” reads as “caretaker and completer of sign-marked realms.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001040/B002","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ff670201c433b668c174","text":"3. **The worlds as signs, not only domains.** Lexical evidence: ع ل م B002, a distinguishing mark that points to something; ه د ي B001 in 1:6, guidance to road/truth; ص ر ط B001 in 1:6–1:7, road. Structural cue: عَٰلَمِينَ sits immediately after رب and later the path request asks for orientation. Assigned role: worlds act as marked surfaces through which lordship becomes knowable and directional. Abductive move: the packet supplies “worlds” through the root علم and “path/guidance” later; I infer that visibility of marks enables movement. Reading change: the ayah’s cosmology becomes semiotic: the worlds are readable signs under رب, not inert scenery.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
